### Shared readiness implementation — local checkpoint, 2026-10-04

Status: **12709a6 manager-accepted as local shared readiness**, following code/diff, actual final document/authority/calendar tests, other focused checks and build review (57 distinct total). This extends accepted eac4a0a instead of rebuilding extraction/retrieval/resolution. Earlier literal-field/single-quote and origin-source-authority descriptions above are historical. Version at acceptance: **document-extract-3/resolve-2/rubric-1 / octo-documents-v3**. No schema/access change or compatibility layer; actual Gemini/provider/rubric and hosted rollout remain open. The next ready author/GFW integration follows immediately, without a preliminary quality study.

**Support retrieval:** processing mode follows one hop from structural primary links, prioritizing explicit registration/organizer relationships, at most three normalized destinations per article and six per run. It reuses the existing public HTTPS/DNS, 24-request, 512-KiB response, three-redirect and deadline safeguards. Requested destinations are deduplicated; identical final captures enter the context once. Support links are not recursively followed. URL/status/MIME/body are captured without wholesale response headers, with stable capture IDs/hashes, versioned cleaned content and the primary-to-support link/label/context. JSON-LD and the observed Zoom JSON registration envelope are retained without executing scripts or interpreting them into record fields. UI/auth/tracking configuration and Zoom's timezone-selection catalogue are explicit omissions; full raw responses remain inspectable. Failed/unsupported/deferred destinations and retained preparation references appear in support_coverage artifacts. The support cache lasts only one run. Every processing invocation rechecks bounded support even when the primary is unchanged; support/coverage hashes enter processing identity so support-only changes become eligible. Fetch/prepare-only mode remains unchanged.

**Combined factual evidence:** proposals now retain document-ID/quote citations, per-field citation indices and normalization rationale. Text need not reproduce source wording. Dates retain local calendar/IANA zone and separate year/date/clock/zone references; deterministic guards check types/keys/lengths, resolvable supplied citations, literal public URLs, explicit cited year, real calendar conversion and ordering. Resolution independently reviews every non-null field and priority as supported/unsupported/conflict and records none/completion/correction/unresolved. Unsupported additions or conflicting facts cannot save and require bounded repair or Uncertain. Neither stage may substitute publication/copyright year or invent missing clock/zone. Semantic faithfulness and component meaning remain model judgments requiring real import/human assessment; local guards do not prove them by regex.

**Confirmed authority defect and correction:** the previous code set authority from `created===1`, then required `authoritative_source` for known-field changes. A secondary source arriving first could therefore block a later genuine registration page. Those flags are removed. Accepted field proofs retain value, relationship rank, source URL/capture/hash and superseded witnesses. A cited document gains registration/organizer rank only from a retained explicit link relationship and a cited matching finding identity. Ordinary primary/secondary documents have no special rank from first arrival or later observation. Compatible evidence can complete unknowns. Established-value changes require same-finding correction citations and a stronger relationship, or a changed payload from the existing factual source; unrelated corroboration does not acquire overwrite authority. Every accepted payload version of the same factual source is retained, including versions that repeat the same value, so a stale payload cannot reverse a later correction. Cited support gets record-owned evidence links while canonical field proofs stay on the primary association. Candidate snapshots, transaction rollback and progress revisions remain enforced; newer observed support also blocks stale saves.

**Packing and deviations:** the repeated evidence schema initially exceeded the 6,000-byte packing allowance even with no source text. Typed object maps retain strict runtime key checks with a smaller schema. Initial extraction now reserves **1,000 bytes**, rather than 2,000, because the latter could not retain minimum primary/support context for saved MPAtlas. The full serialized **8,000-byte hard bound remains unchanged** for initial/repair requests; oversized repairs defer intact. All primary/support text ranges remain traversable with explicit omissions, heading/table/adjacent context and the primary opening 400 characters during support chunks. Remote evidence elsewhere in a long primary may still need a later repair/reassessment or remain uncertain; this is bounded context, not unlimited comprehension. Repeated authority payload hashes remain in stored proofs; repeated identical URL/relationship metadata is sent once per field to avoid needless model-input growth. Two/minute, ten/run, 1,024 output tokens, one counted transient retry and two validation repairs per stage/cycle remain unchanged; no daily cap or paid fallback.

**Verification:** **57 distinct focused tests**: 25 document-stage, 12 preparation, 14 report/access/UI, four hosted boundaries, two public-fetch guards; production build and diff check. The initial combined run passed 54 before the final support-concurrency case; the final 25-document run and affected authority/concurrency rerun supersede overlapping earlier document results, not additional test totals. Actual request serialization/reservations/response handling use injected transport and fresh PGlite. Tests preserve durable reservations while simulating elapsed minutes where needed; separate tests exercise real shared minute/run rejection.

The saved MPAtlas article plus full Zoom registration response produces **2026-10-20 16:00 UTC** using the article's October 20/4 pm UTC and registration's explicit event year, with a faithful concise description and registration authority. All combined text ranges are checked for traversal.
